<h2 class="node-title">
<a href="/article/how-flash-your-netgear-router-dd-wrt-5-easy-steps">How to Flash Your NETGEAR Router with DD-WRT: 5 Easy Steps</a>
</h2>
</div>
<div class="footer submitted">
<div class="user-picture">
<img alt="MyOpenRouter Team's picture" height="40" src="https://www.myopenrouter.com/sites/default/files/styles/img_40_40/public/pictures/picture-3-1464812795.jpg?itok=H9vaRjJk" title="MyOpenRouter Team's picture" width="40"/> </div>
      Submitted by <span class="username">MyOpenRouter Team</span> on <span class="time pubdate" title="2019-09-26T11:32:54-06:00">Thu, 09/26/2019 - 11:32am</span> </div>
<div class="node-content">
<div class="section field field-name-field-thumbnail field-type-image field-label-hidden"><div class="field-items"><div class="field-item odd"><a href="/article/how-flash-your-netgear-router-dd-wrt-5-easy-steps"><img alt="How to Flash DD-WRT to your NETGEAR Router" height="174" src="https://www.myopenrouter.com/sites/default/files/styles/medium/public/question.jpg?itok=b1pWijGw" title="How to Flash DD-WRT to your NETGEAR Router" width="194"/></a></div></div></div><div class="section field field-name-body field-type-text-with-summary field-label-hidden"><div class="field-items"><div class="field-item odd"><p>One common question that we see around the forums is simply: <em><strong>how do I flash my NETGEAR router with DD-WRT?</strong></em> The world of open source firmware but can definitely be overwhelming for newcomers to the scene. Fortunately, flashing DD-WRT to your NETGEAR router is often much easier than it might seem at first, with a few of the terms and steps made clearer. Here's how to get started.</p></div></div></div> </div>
<div class="menu node-links clearfix"><ul class="links inline"><li class="node-readmore first"><a href="/article/how-flash-your-netgear-router-dd-wrt-5-easy-steps" rel="tag" title="How to Flash Your NETGEAR Router with DD-WRT: 5 Easy Steps">Read more<span class="element-invisible"> about How to Flash Your NETGEAR Router with DD-WRT: 5 Easy Steps</span></a></li>

<h2 class="node-title">
<a href="/blog/kong-summer-update-netgear-routers">Kong Summer Update for NETGEAR Routers!</a>
</h2>
</div>
<div class="footer submitted">
<div class="user-picture">
<img alt="Kong's picture" src="https://www.myopenrouter.com/sites/default/files/user-w90h90.gif" title="Kong's picture"/> </div>
      Submitted by <span class="username">Kong</span> on <span class="time pubdate" title="2019-08-06T08:11:35-06:00">Tue, 08/06/2019 - 8:11am</span> </div>
<div class="node-content">
<div class="section field field-name-body field-type-text-with-summary field-label-hidden"><div class="field-items"><div class="field-item odd"><p>If you have not heard, Kong will no longer be releasing DD-WRT builds. You can read his announcement <a href="https://www.desipro.de/dd-wrt/Notice.txt">here</a>.</p>
<p>For those of you who are loyal fans and still want access to Kong's previous builds, we've<a href="https://www.myopenrouter.com/kong-downloads"> created an area here</a> just for you!</p>
<p>Below is a list of his latest summer DD-WRT updates:</p>
<p>EX6200 - Click <a href="https://www.myopenrouter.com/download/ex6200-dd-wrt-kong-summer-firmware-update">here</a>.</p></div></div></div> </div>

<h2 class="node-title">
<a href="/blog/voxel-firmware-updates-custom-netgear-r7800-and-r9000-2019-june">Voxel Firmware Updates Custom for NETGEAR R7800 and R9000  (2019-June)</a>
</h2>
</div>
<div class="footer submitted">
<div class="user-picture">
<img alt="Voxel's picture" height="40" src="https://www.myopenrouter.com/sites/default/files/styles/img_40_40/public/pictures/picture-33194-1479307018.png?itok=vA_0_PbF" title="Voxel's picture" width="40"/> </div>
      Submitted by <span class="username">Voxel</span> on <span class="time pubdate" title="2019-06-14T07:18:25-06:00">Fri, 06/14/2019 - 7:18am</span> </div>
<div class="node-content">
<div class="section field field-name-body field-type-text-with-summary field-label-hidden"><div class="field-items"><div class="field-item odd"><p>Once again Voxel has released new firmware for both the <a class="word-link" href="https://amzn.to/2KYZEcm" target="_blank" title="R7800">R7800</a> and <a class="word-link" href="http://amzn.to/2fVGt09" target="_blank" title="R9000 on Amazon">R9000</a> Nighthawks. Available here are the downloads along with changelogs.</p>
<p> </p>
<p><span style="color: rgb(51, 51, 51); font-family: Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif; font-size: 13px;">Voxel's firmware gives increased functionality that improves the CPU power on your Nighthawk and adds many other powerful features as well!</span></p>

<script type="text/javascript">
<!--//--><![CDATA[//><!--
/**
 * @file
 *   Main JavaScript file for Dismiss module
 */

(function ($) {

  Drupal.behaviors.dismiss = {
    attach: function (context, settings) {

      // Prepend the Dismiss button to each message box.
      $('.messages').each(function () {
        var flag = $(this).children().hasClass('dismiss');

        if (!flag) {
          $(this).prepend('<button class="dismiss"><span class="element-invisible">' + Drupal.t('Close this message.') + '</span></button>');
        }
      });

      // When the Dismiss button is clicked hide this set of messages.
      $('.dismiss').click(function (event) {
        $(this).parent().hide('fast');
        // In case this message is inside a form, prevent form submission.
        event.preventDefault();
      });

      // Fadeout out status messages when positive value defined.
      if (Drupal.settings.dismiss.fadeout > 0) {
        setTimeout(function () { $('.messages.status').fadeOut(); }, Drupal.settings.dismiss.fadeout);
      }

    }
  }

})(jQuery);

//--><!]]>
</script>
